- **D73, gate runs pin their tuple at submit (17:08Z; root cause of P3 31887 dying in 3 s: Agent home moved ef036→a60f8b3 between the head check and the run, so the "current" checkout did not exist):**
  - **Rule:** every P1–P5 request names EXACT SHAs for all four repos, captured once at submit. The service runs THOSE SHAs. It never re-resolves "current home" at run time.

## 2026-10-24T21:50Z ORCHESTRATOR: D84 App item: fix registerAsset ownership (12674) and the placement locator (28255) NOW, source-only, without waiting for Runtime
- **D84, a STRUCTURAL history-verification fix, not more micro-gains (21:50Z; H48 Opus).** So far +5.7%, +4.3% and +8%, and none has closed a gate. Accepted history is fully re-verified on every request, serialized, O(N). Runtime: ONE HARD Sol now (only one Sol is live on main), in the local seconds loop, then ONE security review. In this order:
  1. **Never verify while holding the request/execution lock.**
     - Verify outside it.
     - Re-check only the head pair under the lock (CAS).
     - No cache, so no new trust surface. Try this first.
  2. **Only if (1) is not enough:** an in-process verified-PREFIX memo.
     - Keyed by the authenticated chain position and digest (ordinal plus entry digest of the last verified entry).
     - It verifies only the new suffix, and any mismatch invalidates it and falls back to full verification.
     - It is never persisted.
     - This is exactly the D45/D47 memo class that failed security review before: the reviewer must specifically attack its invalidation (rewind, fork, ABA, restart, tamper).
  - **Gate:** the local loop shows per-request verification time flat in N, then P4 ten-note under 45 s and the App element/popup/parent isolated runs.
- **App:** App-side P2 defects do NOT wait for Runtime. Source-only authors fix registerAsset ownership (12674) and the placement locator (28255) now, under the D74a source-only floor.

- **D89, disk floors re-based now the swings are fixed (01:02Z).** The 0.5–3 GB swings came from git repacks and auto-gc, fixed by LOOPS #76. With those gone, the old margins over-throttle every lane: Agent has 29 reviews pending at local cap 0, and main sits at 6.0 GB.

## 2026-10-25T03:48Z ORCHESTRATOR: D94a (D94 race hole plus H54 actions); act on the items naming your lane
- **D94a, D94 hardening and H54 actions (03:48Z, Opus second opinion):**
  1. **The D94 hole is fixed in the design.** As written, re-recording the baseline (data_version, size) after Runtime's own commit would silently absorb an EXTERNAL write that landed just before it. So:
     - (a) use ONE pinned SQLite connection for the cache, because data_version is per-connection;
     - (b) re-check data_version and file identity INSIDE `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, before the incremental append, and inside every read transaction that uses the cache;
     - (c) file identity is device + inode + size + mtime_ns + ctime_ns for both the DB and the WAL;
     - (d) the D94 author verifies the premise that the Runtime code directory is no less protected than the data directory (same owner and mode). If not, report it, and I will narrow the trade.
     - A **flat local N-curve is required BEFORE the security review starts.** The review brief must include this race.
  2. **Close cost worker 0215.** It is superseded by the cross-process sample and D94.

  4. **Main disk (4.9 GB):** the Runtime manager releases stale main *-pin-* trees by the principles route (the same rule as App and Agent).
